# 증권사 키 종단 간 암호화 동기화 구현 계획

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** 프로필마다 「키도 동기화(암호화)」를 켜면 고른 공급자(KIS·토스)의 키를 동기화 전용 암호로 기기에서 암호화해 서버(D1)에 올리고, 다른 기기는 같은 암호로 받아 그 기기의 키 잠금 설정대로 보관해요. 바뀌면 알리고 고르게 하며, 조용히 덮어쓰지 않아요.

**Architecture:**
- 와이어 형식·상한·검사는 `src/lib/sync/keyWire.ts` 한 곳에 두고 앱과 sync-worker가 함께 써요. 암호 연산(PBKDF2 600000회 → AES-GCM 256, AAD)은 순수 모듈 `src/lib/sync/keyCrypto.ts`가 맡아요. Worker는 암호 연산을 하지 않아요.
- 서버는 별도 「키 금고」 API(`/v1/keys*`, `sync-worker/src/keys.ts`)와 표 셋(`key_vault`·`key_blobs`·`key_guard`, 마이그레이션 0003)을 둬요. 조건은 모두 한 문장 안에 두고, rekey는 감시 표 `key_guard`의 CHECK로 batch 전체를 되돌려요. 프로필 tombstone·복구·정리 batch가 같은 트랜잭션에서 암호문을 삭제 표시로 바꿔요.
- 기기 상태는 새 IndexedDB `gazua-sync-keys`(버전 1, `src/lib/sync/keyStore.ts`)에 둬요. `gazua-sync`의 버전은 올리지 않아요.
- 키 단계(`src/lib/sync/keySync.ts`)는 엔진의 `syncNow = ['pull', 'keys', 'push']` 가운데에서 돌고, 사용자 동작(켜기·받기·올리기·끄기·암호 바꾸기·전체 삭제)은 엔진과 같은 직렬화(`engine.exclusive`) 안에서 해요. KDF는 늘 잠금 밖에서 해요.
- 컨트롤러가 마스터 키(추출 불가 `CryptoKey`)를 탭 메모리에만 두고, 11가지 시점(설계 5.12)에 잊어요. 탭 사이는 `BroadcastChannel('gazua-keys')`와 저장된 세션·소유자 비교로 따라가요.
- 화면은 `src/lib/ui/settings/KeySync.svelte` 하나를 `SyncPanel.svelte` 안(로그인 + 동기화 켜짐)에 둬요.

**Tech Stack:** Svelte 5 runes, TypeScript 5.9, WebCrypto(`globalThis.crypto.subtle`, 새 의존성 없음), idb 8(IndexedDB), Vitest 3(jsdom, fake-indexeddb, @testing-library/svelte, axe-core 4.13.0), Cloudflare Workers + D1(sync-worker, 테스트는 node:sqlite 가짜 D1), Playwright(기존 e2e만).

**근거 문서:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-31-key-sync-design.md`(7회 검토 통과, 커밋 `d67a2b6`). 절 번호는 이 설계를 가리켜요.

**실행 방식:** 각 Task는 그 본문만 읽고 구현할 수 있게 썼어요. 다른 Task의 산출물을 쓰는 곳은 **Interfaces**의 Consumes에 이름과 시그니처를 다시 적었어요. Task는 번호 순서대로 해요.

## 전역 제약

설계가 정한 값을 그대로 옮겨요. 어느 Task에서도 바꾸지 않아요.

- KDF: PBKDF2-SHA256 **600000**회(`KEY_ITER`), salt 16바이트, 결과는 AES-GCM 256 `extractable: false`, 용도 `['encrypt', 'decrypt']`. `iter !== 600000`이면 KDF를 돌기 전에 거절해요. 기기 키 잠금(`secrets.ts`, 310000회)의 형식·회수는 바꾸지 않아요.
- 동기화 암호 강도: `normalize('NFC')` 뒤 code point **12자 이상**, **128자 이하**, **서로 다른 글자 4개 이상**, 확인 입력도 NFC 뒤 비교. 앞뒤 공백은 자르지 않고 안내만 해요. 기기 잠금 암호와 같아도 막지 않아요.
- AAD: 블롭 `gazua-keys/v1|blob|<vaultId>|<profileId>|<revision>|<contentId>|<vaultRevision>`, 확인값 `gazua-keys/v1|verifier|<vaultId>|<vaultRevision>`. 확인값 평문은 `gazua-key-vault-check/v1`(24바이트).
- `FORBIDDEN_KEYS`에 더할 이름(정확히 여덟): `keyVault`, `vaultId`, `keyBlobs`, `nonce`, `ciphertext`, `verifier`, `vaultRevision`, `contentId`. `profileId`·`revision`·`deleted`·`updatedAt`은 금지하지 않아요.
- 크기: base64는 `/^[A-Za-z0-9+/]+={0,2}$/`이고 길이가 4의 배수. `nonce` 16자, `salt`·`vaultId`·`contentId` 24자, 확인값 `ciphertext` 56자, 블롭 `ciphertext` 32~2048자. 자격 값 하나는 `/^[\x21-\x7e]{1,256}$/`. 사용자당 살아 있는 블롭 **50**개. `readJson` 상한: PUT 금고 4 KiB, PUT 블롭 8 KiB, rekey 128 KiB. 두 DELETE(`/v1/keys`, `/v1/keys/:id`)는 본문을 읽지 않아요.
- 리비전: 블롭 `revision`·`vaultRevision`은 `1 ≤ 값 < 2^31`(D1 CHECK). 기기가 보내는 `newRevision`은 `expectedRevision < newRevision ≤ 2^31 − 2`이고 `max(seenRevision, expectedRevision) + 1`로 정해요. 앱은 서버가 준 리비전이 `Number.isSafeInteger`이고 `0 ≤ 값 < 2^31`이 아니면 응답 모양이 틀린 것으로 봐요.
- 행은 금고 전체 삭제(`DELETE /v1/keys`) 때만 지워요. 끄기·tombstone·복구·정리는 암호문(`content_id`·`nonce`·`ciphertext`)만 NULL로 하고 리비전을 + 1 해요.
- 대상: KIS(`appKey`·`appSecret`)·토스(`clientId`·`clientSecret`)만. `proxyToken`·접근 토큰·`tokenIssuedAt`·프록시 주소·데이터 공급자 설정은 올리지 않아요. 받을 때는 블롭에 있고 값이 실제로 다른 공급자만 덮어써요.
- 비밀값 금지: 동기화 암호·마스터 키·`KeyPlainV1`·블롭 암호문·키 해시를 기기 저장소(IndexedDB·localStorage·sessionStorage)·로그·오류 메시지·스냅샷에 두지 않아요. Worker 로그(`deps.log`)는 메서드·패턴·상태·시간만. 오류 응답에 블롭 암호문을 담지 않아요. 테스트의 키 값은 모두 지어낸 값(`FAKE-…`)이에요. 이 문서에도 실제 비밀값을 적지 않아요.
- 문구: 앱 화면 문구는 설계에 적힌 그대로(해요체) 써요. 설계에 없는 문구는 이 계획 「스펙 빈틈」에 적은 것만 써요.
- 자동 받기는 하지 않아요. 받기는 늘 사용자가 눌러요. 토글·받기·올리기는 **지금 프로필**에서만 해요.
- 새 의존성 없음. CSP는 바꾸지 않아요. e2e용 두 번째 빌드는 만들지 않아요(기존 `pnpm e2e`는 통과).

**검증 명령** (저장소 루트에서. 저장소 문서가 `corepack pnpm`을 써서 그대로 따라요)
- `corepack pnpm install`: worktree를 처음 쓸 때 한 번. 워크스페이스(`proxy`·`sync-worker`)까지 의존성을 설치해요. 새 worktree에는 `sync-worker/node_modules`가 없어 `jose`·`vitest`를 못 찾고 Worker 테스트와 `sync-worker/test/harness.ts`를 쓰는 앱 테스트가 실패해요. 새 의존성은 더하지 않아요(lockfile 그대로)
- `corepack pnpm test <파일…>`: 앱 쪽 테스트(`TZ=America/New_York vitest run`). 전체는 `corepack pnpm test`
- `corepack pnpm check`: `svelte-check` 두 tsconfig, 오류 0
- `corepack pnpm --filter sync-worker test [<파일…>]`: Worker 테스트(`vitest run`)
- `corepack pnpm --filter sync-worker check`: `tsc --noEmit`, 오류 0
- `corepack pnpm e2e`: 기존 Playwright(빌드 포함, 몇 분)

**커밋:** 한국어 conventional 형식, 끝에 빈 줄 뒤 `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. 각 Task의 커밋 명령은 `-m` 두 개로 이 형식을 만들어요. 푸시하지 않아요.

## Task 1: 와이어 형식·암호 모듈·금지 키

설계 2.2, 2.3, 2.4, 3.1~3.5, 7.1, 7.2, 7.6(앞 두 항목). 다른 모든 Task가 기대는 순수 모듈이에요.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/lib/sync/keyWire.ts`, `src/lib/sync/keyWire.test.ts`
- Create: `src/lib/sync/keyCrypto.ts`, `src/lib/sync/keyCrypto.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/lib/state/secrets.ts` (`toBase64`·`fromBase64`·`randomBytes` 앞에 `export`)
- Modify: `src/lib/sync/forbidden.ts` (`FORBIDDEN_KEYS`에 여덟 이름)
- Modify: `src/lib/share/testFixtures.ts` (`allKeys`·`shareFixtures` 더하기), `src/lib/share/checkResult.test.ts` (지역 `allKeys`를 지우고 가져오기)
- Test: `src/lib/sync/forbidden.test.ts` (새 describe 더하기)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src/lib/state/secrets.ts`의 `toBase64(bytes: Uint8Array): string`, `fromBase64(b64: string): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>`, `randomBytes(n: number): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>`(이 Task에서 내보냄). `checkProfileDoc(raw: unknown, now?: number): DocCheck`(`src/lib/sync/dto.ts`), `checkShareSnapshot(raw: unknown, kind: unknown, now?: number, mode?: 'create' | 'read'): ShareCheck`(`src/lib/share/snapshot.ts`), `groupSnapshot`·`strategySnapshot`·`criteriaSnapshot`(`snapshot.ts`), `compareSnapshot(g: Group, s: Pick<Settings, 'period' | 'metric' | 'benchmark' | 'boardOrder'>)`(`src/lib/share/compact.ts`), `simResultFixture()`·`validationFixture()`·`NOW`(`testFixtures.ts`), `doc(name?, groups?)`·`T0`(`sync-worker/test/docs.ts`, `sync-worker/test/harness.ts`).
- Produces (`keyWire.ts`):
  - 상수 `KEY_KDF = 'PBKDF2-SHA256'`, `KEY_ITER = 600000`, `KEY_PROVIDERS: readonly KeyProvider[]`, `KEY_BLOBS_MAX = 50`, `NONCE_CHARS = 16`, `ID_CHARS = 24`, `VERIFIER_CIPHERTEXT_CHARS = 56`, `BLOB_CIPHERTEXT_MIN = 32`, `BLOB_CIPHERTEXT_MAX = 2048`, `REVISION_LIMIT = 2 ** 31`, `NEW_REVISION_MAX = 2 ** 31 - 2`, `KEY_BODY_MAX = { vault: 4096, blob: 8192, rekey: 131072 }`, `CREDENTIAL_RE`, `KEY_PROFILE_ID_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,64}$/`
  - 타입 `KeyProvider = 'kis' | 'toss'`, `Sealed { nonce; ciphertext }`, `KeyVaultHead`, `KeyBlobRow`, `KeyBlob`, `KeyPlainV1`, `VaultBody`, `BlobBody`, `RekeyBlob`, `RekeyBody`, `WireCheck<T> = { ok: true; value: T } | { ok: false; code: 'invalid' | 'unknown-field'; path: string }`, `BlobRows { rows: KeyBlobRow[]; skippedIds: string[]; unknownSkipped: boolean }`
  - `isBase64(v: unknown, len?: { exact?: number; min?: number; max?: number }): v is string`, `checkVaultBody(raw: unknown): WireCheck<VaultBody>`, `checkBlobBody(raw: unknown): WireCheck<BlobBody>`, `checkRekeyBody(raw: unknown): WireCheck<RekeyBody>`, `checkVaultHead(raw: unknown): KeyVaultHead | null`, `checkBlobRows(raw: unknown): BlobRows | null`, `checkBlob(raw: unknown): KeyBlob | null`, `checkKeyPlain(raw: unknown): KeyPlainV1 | null`, `keyPlainProviders(p: KeyPlainV1): KeyProvider[]`
- Produces (`keyCrypto.ts`): `VERIFIER_TEXT`, `class KeyOpenError extends Error { readonly reason: 'auth' | 'shape' }`, `interface BlobContext { vaultId: string; profileId: string; revision: number; contentId: string; vaultRevision: number }`, `blobAad(c: BlobContext): string`, `verifierAad(vaultId: string, vaultRevision: number): string`, `randomId(): string`(16바이트 base64 24자), `deriveVaultKey(passphrase: string, salt: string, iter: number): Promise<CryptoKey>`, `makeVerifier(key: CryptoKey, vaultId: string, vaultRevision: number): Promise<Sealed>`, `checkVerifier(key: CryptoKey, v: Sealed, vaultId: string, vaultRevision: number): Promise<boolean>`, `sealKeys(key: CryptoKey, ctx: BlobContext, plain: KeyPlainV1): Promise<Sealed>`, `openKeys(key: CryptoKey, ctx: BlobContext, s: Sealed): Promise<KeyPlainV1>`, `openRaw(key: CryptoKey, ctx: BlobContext, s: Sealed): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>`, `sealRaw(key: CryptoKey, ctx: BlobContext, bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>): Promise<Sealed>`, `type PassphraseProblem = 'short' | 'long' | 'plain' | 'mismatch'`, `PASSPHRASE_TEXT: Record<PassphraseProblem, string>`, `EDGE_SPACE_TEXT`, `checkSyncPassphrase(pass: string, confirm: string): PassphraseProblem | null`, `hasEdgeSpace(pass: string): boolean`, `samePassphrase(a: string, b: string): boolean`
- Produces (`testFixtures.ts`): `allKeys(v: unknown, out?: Set<string>): Set<string>`, `shareFixtures(): ShareSnapshot[]`(여섯 종류, 순서 group·strategy·criteria·compare·simResult·validation)

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트를 써요**

`src/lib/sync/keyWire.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  checkBlob,
  checkBlobBody,
  checkBlobRows,
  checkKeyPlain,
  checkRekeyBody,
  checkVaultBody,
  checkVaultHead,
  KEY_ITER,
  KEY_KDF,
} from './keyWire';

const A = (n: number, c = 'A') => c.repeat(n);
const verifier = { nonce: A(16, 'N'), ciphertext: A(56, 'C') };
const vault = () => ({ vaultId: A(24, 'V'), kdf: KEY_KDF, iter: KEY_ITER, salt: A(24, 'S'), verifier: { ...verifier } });
const blob = () => ({ expectedRevision: 0, newRevision: 1, contentId: A(24, 'I'), vaultId: A(24, 'V'), vaultRevision: 1, nonce: A(16, 'N'), ciphertext: A(64, 'X') });
const rekeyBlob = (profileId: string) => ({ profileId, revision: 1, contentId: A(24, 'I'), nonce: A(16, 'N'), ciphertext: A(64, 'X') });
const rekey = (n = 1) => ({
  expectedVaultId: A(24, 'V'),
  expectedVaultRevision: 1,
  salt: A(24, 'T'),
  verifier: { ...verifier },
  keyBlobs: Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => rekeyBlob(`p${i}`)),
});
const head = () => ({ ...vault(), vaultRevision: 1, updatedAt: 1 });
const row = (profileId: string, o: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({ profileId, revision: 1, vaultRevision: 1, contentId: A(24, 'I'), deleted: false, updatedAt: 1, ...o });

describe('키 와이어 본문 검사', () => {
  it('금고 본문: 정상·모르는 필드·빠진 필드·길이·회수', () => {
    expect(checkVaultBody(vault())).toEqual({ ok: true, value: vault() });
    expect(checkVaultBody({ ...vault(), extra: 1 })).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'unknown-field', path: 'extra' });
    expect(checkVaultBody({ ...vault(), verifier: { ...verifier, x: 1 } })).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'unknown-field', path: 'verifier.x' });
    const { salt: _salt, ...noSalt } = vault();
    expect(checkVaultBody(noSalt)).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'invalid', path: 'salt' });
    expect(checkVaultBody({ ...vault(), vaultId: A(20) })).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'invalid', path: 'vaultId' });
    expect(checkVaultBody({ ...vault(), salt: '!'.repeat(24) })).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'invalid', path: 'salt' });
    expect(checkVaultBody({ ...vault(), iter: 310000 })).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'invalid', path: 'iter' });
    expect(checkVaultBody({ ...vault(), verifier: { ...verifier, ciphertext: A(52) } })).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'invalid', path: 'verifier.ciphertext' });
    expect(checkVaultBody(null)).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'invalid', path: '' });
  });

  it('블롭 본문: 리비전 규칙·암호문 길이·모르는 필드', () => {
    expect(checkBlobBody(blob())).toEqual({ ok: true, value: blob() });
    expect(checkBlobBody({ ...blob(), newRevision: 0 })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, code: 'invalid', path: 'newRevision' });
    expect(checkBlobBody({ ...blob(), expectedRevision: 3, newRevision: 3 })).toMatchObject({ code: 'invalid', path: 'newRevision' });
    expect(checkBlobBody({ ...blob(), expectedRevision: 3, newRevision: 6 }).ok).toBe(true);
    expect(checkBlobBody({ ...blob(), newRevision: 2 ** 31 - 2 }).ok).toBe(true);
    expect(checkBlobBody({ ...blob(), newRevision: 2 ** 31 - 1 })).toMatchObject({ code: 'invalid', path: 'newRevision' });
    expect(checkBlobBody({ ...blob(), expectedRevision: -1 })).toMatchObject({ code: 'invalid', path: 'expectedRevision' });
    expect(checkBlobBody({ ...blob(), ciphertext: A(2048) }).ok).toBe(true);
    expect(checkBlobBody({ ...blob(), ciphertext: A(2052) })).toMatchObject({ code: 'invalid', path: 'ciphertext' });
    expect(checkBlobBody({ ...blob(), ciphertext: A(28) })).toMatchObject({ code: 'invalid', path: 'ciphertext' });
    expect(checkBlobBody({ ...blob(), nonce: A(12) })).toMatchObject({ code: 'invalid', path: 'nonce' });
    expect(checkBlobBody({ ...blob(), vaultRevision: 0 })).toMatchObject({ code: 'invalid', path: 'vaultRevision' });
    expect(checkBlobBody({ ...blob(), proxyToken: 'x' })).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'unknown-field', path: 'proxyToken' });
  });

  it('rekey 본문: 50개까지, 중복 프로필·51개는 invalid', () => {
    expect(checkRekeyBody(rekey(50)).ok).toBe(true);
    expect(checkRekeyBody(rekey(51))).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'invalid', path: 'keyBlobs' });
    const dup = rekey(2);
    dup.keyBlobs[1].profileId = 'p0';
    expect(checkRekeyBody(dup)).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'invalid', path: 'keyBlobs[1].profileId' });
    const extra = rekey(1);
    (extra.keyBlobs[0] as Record<string, unknown>).extra = 1;
    expect(checkRekeyBody(extra)).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'unknown-field', path: 'keyBlobs[0].extra' });
    expect(checkRekeyBody({ ...rekey(0), keyBlobs: [rekeyBlob('../x')] })).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'invalid', path: 'keyBlobs[0].profileId' });
    expect(checkRekeyBody({ ...rekey(1), expectedVaultRevision: 0 })).toMatchObject({ code: 'invalid', path: 'expectedVaultRevision' });
  });
});

describe('서버 응답 검사', () => {
  it('금고 머리: 회수·길이·리비전·모르는 필드가 틀리면 null', () => {
    expect(checkVaultHead(head())).toEqual(head());
    const bads: Record<string, unknown>[] = [
      { iter: 1000 },
      { iter: 1_000_000_000 },
      { vaultId: A(20) },
      { vaultRevision: 0 },
      { vaultRevision: -1 },
      { vaultRevision: 2 ** 31 },
      { vaultRevision: 1.5 },
      { extra: 1 },
    ];
    for (const bad of bads) expect(checkVaultHead({ ...head(), ...bad }), JSON.stringify(bad)).toBeNull();
    expect(checkVaultHead(null)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('블롭 목록: 배열이 아니면 null, 틀린 행만 빠지고 나머지는 쓴다', () => {
    expect(checkBlobRows({})).toBeNull();
    const r = checkBlobRows([
      row('p1'),
      row('p2', { revision: -1 }),
      row('p3', { revision: 2 ** 31 }),
      row('p4', { revision: 1.5 }),
      row('p5', { deleted: true, contentId: null }),
      { revision: 1 },
    ]);
    expect(r).toEqual({ rows: [row('p1'), row('p5', { deleted: true, contentId: null })], skippedIds: ['p2', 'p3', 'p4'], unknownSkipped: true });
    expect(checkBlobRows([row('p1', { deleted: true })])?.skippedIds).toEqual(['p1']);
    expect(checkBlobRows([row('p1'), row('p1')])).toEqual({ rows: [], skippedIds: ['p1'], unknownSkipped: false });
  });

  it('블롭: 모양이 틀리면 null', () => {
    const b = { revision: 1, contentId: A(24, 'I'), vaultRevision: 1, nonce: A(16, 'N'), ciphertext: A(64, 'X'), updatedAt: 1 };
    expect(checkBlob(b)).toEqual(b);
    expect(checkBlob({ ...b, revision: 2 ** 31 })).toBeNull();
    expect(checkBlob({ ...b, ciphertext: A(28) })).toBeNull();
    expect(checkBlob({ ...b, extra: 1 })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('평문: 공급자 하나 이상, 정확한 필드, 출력 가능한 ASCII 256자', () => {
    const kis = { appKey: 'FAKE-kis-key-0001', appSecret: 'FAKE/secret+0001=' };
    expect(checkKeyPlain({ v: 1, kis })).toEqual({ v: 1, kis });
    const bads: unknown[] = [
      { v: 1 },
      { v: 2, kis },
      { v: 1, kis, extra: 1 },
      { v: 1, kis: { ...kis, x: 1 } },
      { v: 1, kis: { ...kis, appKey: '' } },
      { v: 1, kis: { ...kis, appKey: 'a'.repeat(257) } },
      { v: 1, kis: { ...kis, appKey: 'a b' } },
      { v: 1, kis: { ...kis, appKey: '한글' } },
      { v: 1, kis, proxyToken: 'FAKE-proxy' },
    ];
    for (const bad of bads) expect(checkKeyPlain(bad), JSON.stringify(bad)).toBeNull();
    expect(checkKeyPlain({ v: 1, kis: { ...kis, appKey: 'a'.repeat(256) } })).not.toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`src/lib/sync/keyCrypto.test.ts`:

```ts
import { beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import {
  checkSyncPassphrase,
  checkVerifier,
  deriveVaultKey,
  hasEdgeSpace,
  makeVerifier,
  openKeys,
  openRaw,
  randomId,
  sealKeys,
  sealRaw,
  VERIFIER_TEXT,
  type BlobContext,
} from './keyCrypto';
import { KEY_ITER, type KeyPlainV1 } from './keyWire';

// PBKDF2 600000회는 한 번에 0.5초 안팎이라 시간 한도를 늘린다
vi.setConfig({ testTimeout: 60_000, hookTimeout: 60_000 });

const SALT = randomId();
const PASS = 'FAKE-sync-pass-0001';
const ctx: BlobContext = { vaultId: randomId(), profileId: 'p-a', revision: 3, contentId: randomId(), vaultRevision: 1 };
const plain: KeyPlainV1 = { v: 1, kis: { appKey: 'FAKE-kis-key-0001', appSecret: 'FAKE/secret+0001=' } };
let key: CryptoKey;
let other: CryptoKey;

beforeAll(async () => {
  key = await deriveVaultKey(PASS, SALT, KEY_ITER);
  other = await deriveVaultKey(`${PASS}x`, SALT, KEY_ITER);
});

describe('마스터 키', () => {
  it('추출 불가 AES-GCM 256, PBKDF2-SHA256 600000회, 다른 회수는 KDF 전에 거절', async () => {
    expect(key.extractable).toBe(false);
    expect(key.algorithm).toMatchObject({ name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 });
    expect([...key.usages].sort()).toEqual(['decrypt', 'encrypt']);
    const spy = vi.spyOn(globalThis.crypto.subtle, 'deriveKey');
    await deriveVaultKey(PASS, SALT, KEY_ITER);
    expect(spy.mock.calls[0][0]).toMatchObject({ name: 'PBKDF2', iterations: 600000, hash: 'SHA-256' });
    spy.mockClear();
    await expect(deriveVaultKey(PASS, SALT, 599999)).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(deriveVaultKey(PASS, SALT, 600001)).rejects.toThrow();
    expect(spy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    spy.mockRestore();
  });

  it('NFC: 조합형과 분해형 암호가 같은 키를 만든다', async () => {
    const nfc = '한글로-된-동기화-암호';
    const nfd = nfc.normalize('NFD');
    expect(nfd).not.toBe(nfc);
    const k1 = await deriveVaultKey(nfc, SALT, KEY_ITER);
    const k2 = await deriveVaultKey(nfd, SALT, KEY_ITER);
    expect(await openKeys(k2, ctx, await sealKeys(k1, ctx, plain))).toEqual(plain);
    expect(checkSyncPassphrase(nfc, nfd)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('확인값', () => {
  it('맞는 암호만 true, 다른 금고·리비전·위조는 예외 없이 false', async () => {
    expect(new TextEncoder().encode(VERIFIER_TEXT).length).toBe(24);
    const v = await makeVerifier(key, ctx.vaultId, 1);
    expect(v.nonce).toHaveLength(16);
    expect(v.ciphertext).toHaveLength(56);
    expect(await checkVerifier(key, v, ctx.vaultId, 1)).toBe(true);
    expect(await checkVerifier(other, v, ctx.vaultId, 1)).toBe(false);
    expect(await checkVerifier(key, v, randomId(), 1)).toBe(false);
    expect(await checkVerifier(key, v, ctx.vaultId, 2)).toBe(false);
    const forged = { ...v, ciphertext: (v.ciphertext[0] === 'A' ? 'B' : 'A') + v.ciphertext.slice(1) };
    expect(await checkVerifier(key, forged, ctx.vaultId, 1)).toBe(false);
    expect(await checkVerifier(key, { nonce: '!!', ciphertext: '??' }, ctx.vaultId, 1)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('블롭', () => {
  it('AAD: 다른 프로필·금고·리비전·내용 ID·금고 리비전·키로는 auth 실패', async () => {
    const s = await sealKeys(key, ctx, plain);
    expect(await openKeys(key, ctx, s)).toEqual(plain);
    const others: BlobContext[] = [
      { ...ctx, profileId: 'p-b' },
      { ...ctx, vaultId: randomId() },
      { ...ctx, revision: 4 },
      { ...ctx, contentId: randomId() },
      { ...ctx, vaultRevision: 2 },
    ];
    for (const c of others) await expect(openKeys(key, c, s)).rejects.toMatchObject({ name: 'KeyOpenError', reason: 'auth' });
    await expect(openKeys(other, ctx, s)).rejects.toMatchObject({ name: 'KeyOpenError', reason: 'auth' });
  });

  it('평문 모양이 틀리면 shape, sealKeys도 공급자 없는 평문·proxyToken·2048자 초과를 거절', async () => {
    const raw = (v: unknown) => sealRaw(key, ctx, new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(v)));
    const bads: unknown[] = [
      { v: 1 },
      { v: 1, kis: plain.kis, extra: 1 },
      { v: 1, kis: { appKey: '', appSecret: 'FAKE' } },
      { v: 1, kis: { appKey: 'a'.repeat(257), appSecret: 'FAKE' } },
      { v: 1, kis: { appKey: 'a b', appSecret: 'FAKE' } },
      { v: 1, kis: { appKey: '한글', appSecret: 'FAKE' } },
      { v: 1, kis: plain.kis, proxyToken: 'FAKE-proxy' },
    ];
    for (const bad of bads) await expect(openKeys(key, ctx, await raw(bad))).rejects.toMatchObject({ reason: 'shape' });
    await expect(sealKeys(key, ctx, { v: 1 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ reason: 'shape' });
    // @ts-expect-error proxyToken은 KeyPlainV1에 없다
    await expect(sealKeys(key, ctx, { v: 1, kis: plain.kis, proxyToken: 'FAKE-proxy' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ reason: 'shape' });
    // 봉한 암호문이 2048자를 넘으면 거절(sealKeys·sealRaw가 같은 봉하기를 쓴다)
    await expect(sealRaw(key, ctx, new Uint8Array(new ArrayBuffer(1600)))).rejects.toMatchObject({ reason: 'shape' });
  });

  it('openRaw·sealRaw: 모르는 필드가 든 평문도 바이트 그대로 다시 봉한다', async () => {
    const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify({ v: 1, kis: plain.kis, future: { x: 1 } }));
    const s = await sealRaw(key, ctx, bytes);
    const opened = await openRaw(key, ctx, s);
    expect(new TextDecoder().decode(opened)).toBe(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes));
    const next = { ...ctx, vaultRevision: 2 };
    const again = await sealRaw(other, next, opened);
    expect(new TextDecoder().decode(await openRaw(other, next, again))).toBe(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes));
  });
});

describe('동기화 암호 강도', () => {
  it('12자·128자·서로 다른 글자 4개·확인 일치, 서로게이트 쌍은 한 글자', () => {
    expect(checkSyncPassphrase('abcdefghijk', 'abcdefghijk')).toBe('short');
    expect(checkSyncPassphrase('abcdefghijkl', 'abcdefghijkl')).toBeNull();
    const long = 'abcd'.repeat(32) + 'e';
    expect(checkSyncPassphrase(long, long)).toBe('long');
    expect(checkSyncPassphrase('aaaaaaaaaaaa', 'aaaaaaaaaaaa')).toBe('plain');
    expect(checkSyncPassphrase('121212121212', '121212121212')).toBe('plain');
    expect(checkSyncPassphrase('abcdefghijkl', 'abcdefghijkm')).toBe('mismatch');
    expect(checkSyncPassphrase('😀'.repeat(11), '😀'.repeat(11))).toBe('short');
    expect(checkSyncPassphrase('😀'.repeat(12), '😀'.repeat(12))).toBe('plain');
    const emoji = '😀😁😂🤣'.repeat(3);
    expect(checkSyncPassphrase(emoji, emoji)).toBeNull();
    expect(hasEdgeSpace(' abcdefghijkl')).toBe(true);
    expect(hasEdgeSpace('abcdef ghijkl')).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`src/lib/sync/forbidden.test.ts`: 기존 `./forbidden` import를 `import { exceedsDepth, findForbidden, FORBIDDEN_KEYS, JSON_DEPTH_MAX, jsonDiff } from './forbidden';`로 바꾸고, 그 아래에 import를 더한 뒤 파일 끝에 describe를 더해요.

```ts
import { checkShareSnapshot } from '../share/snapshot';
import { allKeys, NOW as SHARE_NOW, shareFixtures } from '../share/testFixtures';
import { doc } from '../../../sync-worker/test/docs';
import { T0 } from '../../../sync-worker/test/harness';
import { checkProfileDoc } from './dto';

const KEY_WIRE_NAMES = ['keyVault', 'vaultId', 'keyBlobs', 'nonce', 'ciphertext', 'verifier', 'vaultRevision', 'contentId'];

/** 값 안에서 가장 깊은 객체(배열 제외)의 경로 조각. 루트면 [] */
function deepestObject(v: unknown, path: (string | number)[] = [], best = { depth: -1, path: [] as (string | number)[] }) {
  if (Array.isArray(v)) v.forEach((x, i) => deepestObject(x, [...path, i], best));
  else if (typeof v === 'object' && v !== null) {
    if (path.length > best.depth) Object.assign(best, { depth: path.length, path });
    for (const [k, x] of Object.entries(v)) deepestObject(x, [...path, k], best);
  }
  return best.path;
}
function inject(v: unknown, at: (string | number)[], key: string): string {
  let o = v as Record<string | number, unknown>;
  for (const k of at) o = o[k] as Record<string | number, unknown>;
  o[key] = 'FAKE';
  return at.reduce<string>((p, k) => (typeof k === 'number' ? `${p}[${k}]` : p ? `${p}.${k}` : k), '');
}

describe('키 동기화 와이어 이름 금지(설계 2.4, 7.6)', () => {
  it('여덟 이름이 FORBIDDEN_KEYS에 있고, profileId·revision·deleted·updatedAt은 없다', () => {
    for (const k of KEY_WIRE_NAMES) expect(FORBIDDEN_KEYS, k).toContain(k);
    for (const k of ['profileId', 'revision', 'deleted', 'updatedAt']) expect(FORBIDDEN_KEYS, k).not.toContain(k);
  });

  it('프로필 문서 깊은 자리의 keyVault·nonce는 forbidden-field와 그 경로', () => {
    const a = doc() as unknown as { watchlists: { groups: Record<string, unknown>[] }; strategies: Record<string, unknown>[] };
    a.watchlists.groups[0].keyVault = { x: 1 };
    expect(checkProfileDoc(a, T0)).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'forbidden-field', path: 'watchlists.groups[0].keyVault' });
    const b = doc() as unknown as { strategies: Record<string, unknown>[] };
    b.strategies[0].Nonce = 'FAKE';
    expect(checkProfileDoc(b, T0)).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'forbidden-field', path: 'strategies[0].Nonce' });
  });

  it('공유 여섯 종류마다 가장 깊은 객체에 ciphertext·verifier를 넣으면 forbidden-field', () => {
    for (const name of ['ciphertext', 'verifier']) {
      for (const fixture of shareFixtures()) {
        expect(checkShareSnapshot(fixture, fixture.kind, SHARE_NOW).ok, fixture.kind).toBe(true);
        const s = structuredClone(fixture);
        const at = deepestObject(s);
        const base = inject(s, at, name);
        const path = base ? `${base}.${name}` : name;
        expect(checkShareSnapshot(s, s.kind, SHARE_NOW), `${s.kind} ${path}`).toEqual({ ok: false, code: 'forbidden-field', path });
      }
    }
  });

  it('정상 고정 데이터(프로필 문서 + 여섯 공유 종류)의 키 경로는 FORBIDDEN_KEYS와 겹치지 않는다', () => {
    const forbidden = new Set(FORBIDDEN_KEYS.map((k) => k.toLowerCase()));
    const keys = new Set<string>([...allKeys(doc())]);
    for (const s of shareFixtures()) allKeys(s, keys);
    expect(keys.size).toBeGreaterThan(30);
    for (const k of keys) expect(forbidden.has(k.toLowerCase()), k).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`src/lib/sync/keyWire.ts`:

```ts
// 키 동기화 와이어 형식(docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-31-key-sync-design.md 2.2·2.3). 앱과 sync-worker가 함께 쓴다.
// DOM·import.meta.env·저장소를 쓰지 않는다(dto.ts와 같은 규칙). 오류에는 경로만 담고 값은 담지 않는다.

export const KEY_KDF = 'PBKDF2-SHA256';
export const KEY_ITER = 600000;
export type KeyProvider = 'kis' | 'toss';
export const KEY_PROVIDERS: readonly KeyProvider[] = ['kis', 'toss'];
/** 사용자당 살아 있는 블롭 상한(2.3) */
export const KEY_BLOBS_MAX = 50;
export const NONCE_CHARS = 16;
/** salt·vaultId·contentId(16바이트) */
export const ID_CHARS = 24;
/** 확인값 암호문: 고정 문구 24바이트 + 태그 16바이트 */
export const VERIFIER_CIPHERTEXT_CHARS = 56;
export const BLOB_CIPHERTEXT_MIN = 32;
export const BLOB_CIPHERTEXT_MAX = 2048;
/** 리비전·금고 리비전은 이 값 미만(D1 CHECK) */
export const REVISION_LIMIT = 2 ** 31;
/** 기기가 보낼 수 있는 가장 큰 newRevision(4.5) */
export const NEW_REVISION_MAX = 2 ** 31 - 2;
/** readJson 상한(2.3). 두 DELETE는 본문을 읽지 않는다 */
export const KEY_BODY_MAX = { vault: 4 * 1024, blob: 8 * 1024, rekey: 128 * 1024 } as const;
/** 자격 값 하나: 출력 가능한 ASCII 1~256자 */
export const CREDENTIAL_RE = /^[\x21-\x7e]{1,256}$/;
/** 프로필 ID(sync-worker profiles.ts의 checkId와 같은 규칙) */
export const KEY_PROFILE_ID_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,64}$/;
const BASE64_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9+/]+={0,2}$/;

/** AES-GCM 한 번의 결과. 모두 표준 base64(패딩 포함) */
export interface Sealed {
  nonce: string;
  ciphertext: string;
}
/** GET /v1/keys의 금고 머리 */
export interface KeyVaultHead {
  vaultId: string;
  kdf: typeof KEY_KDF;
  iter: typeof KEY_ITER;
  salt: string;
  verifier: Sealed;
  vaultRevision: number;
  updatedAt: number;
}
export interface KeyBlobRow {
  profileId: string;
  revision: number;
  vaultRevision: number;
  /** 살아 있는 행의 내용 ID(비밀 아님). 삭제 표시면 null */
  contentId: string | null;
  /** true면 삭제 표시(끄기·프로필 tombstone). 암호문이 없다 */
  deleted: boolean;
  updatedAt: number;
}
export interface KeyBlob extends Sealed {
  revision: number;
  contentId: string;
  vaultRevision: number;
  updatedAt: number;
}
/** 블롭을 풀었을 때의 평문. 서버로 가지 않는다 */
export interface KeyPlainV1 {
  v: 1;
  kis?: { appKey: string; appSecret: string };
  toss?: { clientId: string; clientSecret: string };
}
export interface VaultBody {
  vaultId: string;
  kdf: typeof KEY_KDF;
  iter: typeof KEY_ITER;
  salt: string;
  verifier: Sealed;
}
export interface BlobBody extends Sealed {
  expectedRevision: number;
  newRevision: number;
  contentId: string;
  vaultId: string;
  vaultRevision: number;
}
export interface RekeyBlob extends Sealed {
  profileId: string;
  revision: number;
  contentId: string;
}
export interface RekeyBody {
  expectedVaultId: string;
  expectedVaultRevision: number;
  salt: string;
  verifier: Sealed;
  keyBlobs: RekeyBlob[];
}
export type WireCheck<T> = { ok: true; value: T } | { ok: false; code: 'invalid' | 'unknown-field'; path: string };
/** 목록 검사 결과. 모양이 틀린 행은 빠지고, 그 프로필 ID(읽을 수 있으면)를 skippedIds에 둔다 */
export interface BlobRows {
  rows: KeyBlobRow[];
  skippedIds: string[];
  /** 프로필 ID조차 읽을 수 없는 행이 있었다 */
  unknownSkipped: boolean;
}

export function isBase64(v: unknown, len: { exact?: number; min?: number; max?: number } = {}): v is string {
  if (typeof v !== 'string' || v.length % 4 !== 0 || !BASE64_RE.test(v)) return false;
  if (len.exact !== undefined && v.length !== len.exact) return false;
  if (len.min !== undefined && v.length < len.min) return false;
  if (len.max !== undefined && v.length > len.max) return false;
  return true;
}

const isRecord = (v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> => typeof v === 'object' && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v);
const isRevision = (v: unknown, min = 0): v is number => typeof v === 'number' && Number.isSafeInteger(v) && v >= min && v < REVISION_LIMIT;
const isTime = (v: unknown): v is number => typeof v === 'number' && Number.isSafeInteger(v) && v >= 0;
const join = (path: string, k: string) => (path ? `${path}.${k}` : k);

class Bad {
  constructor(
    readonly code: 'invalid' | 'unknown-field',
    readonly path: string,
  ) {}
}
function fields(o: unknown, allowed: readonly string[], path: string): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (!isRecord(o)) throw new Bad('invalid', path);
  for (const k of Object.keys(o)) if (!allowed.includes(k)) throw new Bad('unknown-field', join(path, k));
  return o;
}
function need(ok: boolean, path: string): void {
  if (!ok) throw new Bad('invalid', path);
}
function sealed(o: Record<string, unknown>, path: string, ct: { exact?: number; min?: number; max?: number }): Sealed {
  need(isBase64(o.nonce, { exact: NONCE_CHARS }), join(path, 'nonce'));
  need(isBase64(o.ciphertext, ct), join(path, 'ciphertext'));
  return { nonce: o.nonce as string, ciphertext: o.ciphertext as string };
}
function verifierOf(v: unknown, path: string): Sealed {
  return sealed(fields(v, ['nonce', 'ciphertext'], path), path, { exact: VERIFIER_CIPHERTEXT_CHARS });
}
const BLOB_CT = { min: BLOB_CIPHERTEXT_MIN, max: BLOB_CIPHERTEXT_MAX };
function run<T>(f: () => T): WireCheck<T> {
  try {
    return { ok: true, value: f() };
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof Bad) return { ok: false, code: e.code, path: e.path };
    throw e;
  }
}

/** PUT /v1/keys/vault 본문(4.4) */
export function checkVaultBody(raw: unknown): WireCheck<VaultBody> {
  return run(() => {
    const o = fields(raw, ['vaultId', 'kdf', 'iter', 'salt', 'verifier'], '');
    need(isBase64(o.vaultId, { exact: ID_CHARS }), 'vaultId');
    need(o.kdf === KEY_KDF, 'kdf');
    need(o.iter === KEY_ITER, 'iter');
    need(isBase64(o.salt, { exact: ID_CHARS }), 'salt');
    const verifier = verifierOf(o.verifier, 'verifier');
    return { vaultId: o.vaultId as string, kdf: KEY_KDF, iter: KEY_ITER, salt: o.salt as string, verifier };
  });
}

/** PUT /v1/keys/:profileId 본문(4.5) */
export function checkBlobBody(raw: unknown): WireCheck<BlobBody> {
  return run(() => {
    const o = fields(raw, ['expectedRevision', 'newRevision', 'contentId', 'vaultId', 'vaultRevision', 'nonce', 'ciphertext'], '');
    need(isRevision(o.expectedRevision), 'expectedRevision');
    const expected = o.expectedRevision as number;
    need(isRevision(o.newRevision, 1) && (o.newRevision as number) > expected && (o.newRevision as number) <= NEW_REVISION_MAX, 'newRevision');
    need(isBase64(o.contentId, { exact: ID_CHARS }), 'contentId');
    need(isBase64(o.vaultId, { exact: ID_CHARS }), 'vaultId');
    need(isRevision(o.vaultRevision, 1), 'vaultRevision');
    const s = sealed(o, '', BLOB_CT);
    return {
      expectedRevision: expected,
      newRevision: o.newRevision as number,
      contentId: o.contentId as string,
      vaultId: o.vaultId as string,
      vaultRevision: o.vaultRevision as number,
      ...s,
    };
  });
}

/** POST /v1/keys/rekey 본문(4.7) */
export function checkRekeyBody(raw: unknown): WireCheck<RekeyBody> {
  return run(() => {
    const o = fields(raw, ['expectedVaultId', 'expectedVaultRevision', 'salt', 'verifier', 'keyBlobs'], '');
    need(isBase64(o.expectedVaultId, { exact: ID_CHARS }), 'expectedVaultId');
    need(isRevision(o.expectedVaultRevision, 1), 'expectedVaultRevision');
    need(isBase64(o.salt, { exact: ID_CHARS }), 'salt');
    const verifier = verifierOf(o.verifier, 'verifier');
    need(Array.isArray(o.keyBlobs) && o.keyBlobs.length <= KEY_BLOBS_MAX, 'keyBlobs');
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    const keyBlobs = (o.keyBlobs as unknown[]).map((b, i): RekeyBlob => {
      const p = `keyBlobs[${i}]`;
      const x = fields(b, ['profileId', 'revision', 'contentId', 'nonce', 'ciphertext'], p);
      need(typeof x.profileId === 'string' && KEY_PROFILE_ID_RE.test(x.profileId) && !seen.has(x.profileId), `${p}.profileId`);
      seen.add(x.profileId as string);
      need(isRevision(x.revision, 1), `${p}.revision`);
      need(isBase64(x.contentId, { exact: ID_CHARS }), `${p}.contentId`);
      const s = sealed(x, p, BLOB_CT);
      return { profileId: x.profileId as string, revision: x.revision as number, contentId: x.contentId as string, ...s };
    });
    return {
      expectedVaultId: o.expectedVaultId as string,
      expectedVaultRevision: o.expectedVaultRevision as number,
      salt: o.salt as string,
      verifier,
      keyBlobs,
    };
  });
}

/** 앱이 받은 금고 머리. 모양이 틀리거나 iter가 600000이 아니면 null(키 단계 전체를 멈춘다, 2.2) */
export function checkVaultHead(raw: unknown): KeyVaultHead | null {
  const r = run((): KeyVaultHead => {
    const o = fields(raw, ['vaultId', 'kdf', 'iter', 'salt', 'verifier', 'vaultRevision', 'updatedAt'], '');
    need(isBase64(o.vaultId, { exact: ID_CHARS }), 'vaultId');
    need(o.kdf === KEY_KDF, 'kdf');
    need(o.iter === KEY_ITER, 'iter');
    need(isBase64(o.salt, { exact: ID_CHARS }), 'salt');
    const verifier = verifierOf(o.verifier, 'verifier');
    need(isRevision(o.vaultRevision, 1), 'vaultRevision');
    need(isTime(o.updatedAt), 'updatedAt');
    return { vaultId: o.vaultId as string, kdf: KEY_KDF, iter: KEY_ITER, salt: o.salt as string, verifier, vaultRevision: o.vaultRevision as number, updatedAt: o.updatedAt as number };
  });
  return r.ok ? r.value : null;
}

function checkBlobRow(raw: unknown): KeyBlobRow | null {
  const r = run((): KeyBlobRow => {
    const o = fields(raw, ['profileId', 'revision', 'vaultRevision', 'contentId', 'deleted', 'updatedAt'], '');
    need(typeof o.profileId === 'string' && KEY_PROFILE_ID_RE.test(o.profileId), 'profileId');
    need(isRevision(o.revision, 1), 'revision');
    need(isRevision(o.vaultRevision, 1), 'vaultRevision');
    need(typeof o.deleted === 'boolean', 'deleted');
    need(o.deleted ? o.contentId === null : isBase64(o.contentId, { exact: ID_CHARS }), 'contentId');
    need(isTime(o.updatedAt), 'updatedAt');
    return {
      profileId: o.profileId as string,
      revision: o.revision as number,
      vaultRevision: o.vaultRevision as number,
      contentId: (o.contentId as string | null) ?? null,
      deleted: o.deleted as boolean,
      updatedAt: o.updatedAt as number,
    };
  });
  return r.ok ? r.value : null;
}

/** 앱이 받은 블롭 목록. 배열이 아니면 null. 행 하나가 틀리면 그 행만 뺀다. 같은 프로필이 두 번 나오면 둘 다 뺀다 */
export function checkBlobRows(raw: unknown): BlobRows | null {
  if (!Array.isArray(raw)) return null;
  const out: BlobRows = { rows: [], skippedIds: [], unknownSkipped: false };
  const count = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const v of raw) if (isRecord(v) && typeof v.profileId === 'string') count.set(v.profileId, (count.get(v.profileId) ?? 0) + 1);
  for (const v of raw) {
    const id = isRecord(v) && typeof v.profileId === 'string' ? v.profileId : null;
    if (id === null) {
      out.unknownSkipped = true;
      continue;
    }
    const r = count.get(id) === 1 ? checkBlobRow(v) : null;
    if (r) out.rows.push(r);
    else if (!out.skippedIds.includes(id)) out.skippedIds.push(id);
  }
  return out;
}

/** 앱이 받은 블롭(GET /v1/keys/:id). 틀리면 null */
export function checkBlob(raw: unknown): KeyBlob | null {
  const r = run((): KeyBlob => {
    const o = fields(raw, ['revision', 'contentId', 'vaultRevision', 'nonce', 'ciphertext', 'updatedAt'], '');
    need(isRevision(o.revision, 1), 'revision');
    need(isBase64(o.contentId, { exact: ID_CHARS }), 'contentId');
    need(isRevision(o.vaultRevision, 1), 'vaultRevision');
    need(isTime(o.updatedAt), 'updatedAt');
    const s = sealed(o, '', BLOB_CT);
    return { revision: o.revision as number, contentId: o.contentId as string, vaultRevision: o.vaultRevision as number, updatedAt: o.updatedAt as number, ...s };
  });
  return r.ok ? r.value : null;
}

const isCredential = (v: unknown): v is string => typeof v === 'string' && CREDENTIAL_RE.test(v);

/** 평문 모양(2.2): 정확한 키 집합, 공급자 하나 이상, 자격 값 규칙(2.3). 아니면 null */
export function checkKeyPlain(raw: unknown): KeyPlainV1 | null {
  if (!isRecord(raw) || raw.v !== 1) return null;
  if (Object.keys(raw).some((k) => k !== 'v' && k !== 'kis' && k !== 'toss')) return null;
  const out: KeyPlainV1 = { v: 1 };
  if (raw.kis !== undefined) {
    const k = raw.kis;
    if (!isRecord(k) || Object.keys(k).some((x) => x !== 'appKey' && x !== 'appSecret')) return null;
    if (!isCredential(k.appKey) || !isCredential(k.appSecret)) return null;
    out.kis = { appKey: k.appKey, appSecret: k.appSecret };
  }
  if (raw.toss !== undefined) {
    const t = raw.toss;
    if (!isRecord(t) || Object.keys(t).some((x) => x !== 'clientId' && x !== 'clientSecret')) return null;
    if (!isCredential(t.clientId) || !isCredential(t.clientSecret)) return null;
    out.toss = { clientId: t.clientId, clientSecret: t.clientSecret };
  }
  return out.kis || out.toss ? out : null;
}

/** 평문에 든 공급자 집합(= 선택, 결정 9) */
export const keyPlainProviders = (p: KeyPlainV1): KeyProvider[] => KEY_PROVIDERS.filter((x) => p[x] !== undefined);
```

`src/lib/sync/keyCrypto.ts`:

```ts
// 키 동기화 암호화(설계 3절). 순수 모듈: globalThis.crypto.subtle만 쓴다. 메시지에 값을 넣지 않는다.
// 기기 키 잠금(secrets.ts, 310000회)과 다른 암호·다른 KDF 회수(600000회)를 쓴다.
import { fromBase64, randomBytes, toBase64 } from '../state/secrets';
import { BLOB_CIPHERTEXT_MAX, checkKeyPlain, KEY_ITER, type KeyPlainV1, type Sealed } from './keyWire';

/** 확인값의 고정 문구(24바이트) */
export const VERIFIER_TEXT = 'gazua-key-vault-check/v1';
const IV_BYTES = 12;
const ID_BYTES = 16;

/** auth: 복호화 실패(틀린 키·AAD·위조), shape: 평문 모양이 틀림·봉한 결과가 상한을 넘음 */
export class KeyOpenError extends Error {
  constructor(readonly reason: 'auth' | 'shape') {
    super(`key blob ${reason}`);
    this.name = 'KeyOpenError';
  }
}

export interface BlobContext {
  vaultId: string;
  profileId: string;
  revision: number;
  contentId: string;
  vaultRevision: number;
}

const encoder = new TextEncoder();
export const blobAad = (c: BlobContext): string =>
  `gazua-keys/v1|blob|${c.vaultId}|${c.profileId}|${c.revision}|${c.contentId}|${c.vaultRevision}`;
export const verifierAad = (vaultId: string, vaultRevision: number): string => `gazua-keys/v1|verifier|${vaultId}|${vaultRevision}`;

/** 금고 ID·salt·내용 ID용 무작위 16바이트(base64 24자) */
export const randomId = (): string => toBase64(randomBytes(ID_BYTES));

export async function deriveVaultKey(passphrase: string, salt: string, iter: number): Promise<CryptoKey> {
  // 서버가 회수를 낮추거나 키워서 기기를 흔들지 못하게 KDF 전에 거절한다(3.1)
  if (iter !== KEY_ITER) throw new Error('unsupported key derivation iteration count');
  const { subtle } = globalThis.crypto;
  const material = await subtle.importKey('raw', encoder.encode(passphrase.normalize('NFC')), 'PBKDF2', false, ['deriveKey']);
  return subtle.deriveKey(
    { name: 'PBKDF2', salt: fromBase64(salt), iterations: iter, hash: 'SHA-256' },
    material,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false,
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt'],
  );
}

async function seal(key: CryptoKey, aad: string, bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>): Promise<Sealed> {
  const iv = randomBytes(IV_BYTES);
  const out = await globalThis.crypto.subtle.encrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv, additionalData: encoder.encode(aad) }, key, bytes);
  const s = { nonce: toBase64(iv), ciphertext: toBase64(new Uint8Array(out)) };
  if (s.ciphertext.length > BLOB_CIPHERTEXT_MAX) throw new KeyOpenError('shape');
  return s;
}

async function open(key: CryptoKey, aad: string, s: Sealed): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>> {
  try {
    const out = await globalThis.crypto.subtle.decrypt(
      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: fromBase64(s.nonce), additionalData: encoder.encode(aad) },
      key,
      fromBase64(s.ciphertext),
    );
    return new Uint8Array(out);
  } catch {
    throw new KeyOpenError('auth');
  }
}

export function makeVerifier(key: CryptoKey, vaultId: string, vaultRevision: number): Promise<Sealed> {
  return seal(key, verifierAad(vaultId, vaultRevision), encoder.encode(VERIFIER_TEXT));
}

/** 틀린 암호·다른 금고·위조면 false. 예외를 밖으로 던지지 않는다 */
export async function checkVerifier(key: CryptoKey, v: Sealed, vaultId: string, vaultRevision: number): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const bytes = await open(key, verifierAad(vaultId, vaultRevision), v);
    return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes) === VERIFIER_TEXT;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export async function sealKeys(key: CryptoKey, ctx: BlobContext, plain: KeyPlainV1): Promise<Sealed> {
  const checked = checkKeyPlain(plain);
  if (!checked) throw new KeyOpenError('shape');
  return seal(key, blobAad(ctx), encoder.encode(JSON.stringify(checked)));
}

export async function openKeys(key: CryptoKey, ctx: BlobContext, s: Sealed): Promise<KeyPlainV1> {
  const bytes = await open(key, blobAad(ctx), s);
  let v: unknown;
  try {
    v = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(bytes));
  } catch {
    throw new KeyOpenError('shape');
  }
  const plain = checkKeyPlain(v);
  if (!plain) throw new KeyOpenError('shape');
  return plain;
}

/** rekey 전용: 평문 모양을 보지 않고 바이트만 푼다(새 버전 앱이 만든 블롭도 다시 봉할 수 있게) */
export function openRaw(key: CryptoKey, ctx: BlobContext, s: Sealed): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>> {
  return open(key, blobAad(ctx), s);
}

export function sealRaw(key: CryptoKey, ctx: BlobContext, bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>): Promise<Sealed> {
  return seal(key, blobAad(ctx), bytes);
}

export type PassphraseProblem = 'short' | 'long' | 'plain' | 'mismatch';
export const PASSPHRASE_TEXT: Record<PassphraseProblem, string> = {
  short: '동기화 암호를 12자 이상 입력해 주세요',
  long: '동기화 암호는 128자까지예요',
  plain: '같은 글자를 되풀이한 암호는 쓸 수 없어요',
  mismatch: '암호가 서로 달라요',
};
/** 오류가 아닌 안내(3.4) */
export const EDGE_SPACE_TEXT = '앞이나 뒤에 공백이 있어요';

/** 3.4 표의 순서대로 본다. 앞뒤 공백은 자르지 않는다 */
export function checkSyncPassphrase(pass: string, confirm: string): PassphraseProblem | null {
  const p = pass.normalize('NFC');
  const c = confirm.normalize('NFC');
  const chars = [...p];
  if (chars.length < 12) return 'short';
  if (chars.length > 128) return 'long';
  if (new Set(chars).size < 4) return 'plain';
  if (p !== c) return 'mismatch';
  return null;
}
export const hasEdgeSpace = (pass: string): boolean => pass !== pass.trim();
export const samePassphrase = (a: string, b: string): boolean => a.normalize('NFC') === b.normalize('NFC');
```

export function allKeys(v: unknown, out = new Set<string>()): Set<string> {
  if (Array.isArray(v)) v.forEach((x) => allKeys(x, out));
  else if (typeof v === 'object' && v !== null) for (const [k, x] of Object.entries(v)) (out.add(k), allKeys(x, out));

}

- Consumes: `checkVaultBody`·`checkBlobBody`·`checkRekeyBody`·`KEY_BLOBS_MAX`·`KEY_BODY_MAX`·`KEY_ITER`·`KEY_KDF`·`WireCheck`·`KeyVaultHead`·`KeyBlobRow`·`KeyBlob`(Task 1, `src/lib/sync/keyWire.ts`, Worker는 `../../src/lib/sync/keyWire`로 가져와요). `checkId(id: string): string`(`sync-worker/src/profiles.ts`, 규칙 `/^[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,64}$/`, 아니면 404). `HttpError(status, code, extra?)`·`readJson(req, max?)`(`@sch0718/firebase-session/worker`). `AuthContext { userId: string; … }`(`sync-worker/src/session.ts`). 테스트: `harness()`·`OTHER`·`Caller`(`sync-worker/test/harness.ts`), `doc()`(`sync-worker/test/docs.ts`), `h.env.DB.raw`(node:sqlite `DatabaseSync`).
- Produces (`sync-worker/src/keySql.ts`): `KEY_TOMBSTONE_SQL: string` — `UPDATE key_blobs SET content_id = NULL, nonce = NULL, ciphertext = NULL, revision = revision + 1, updated_at = ? WHERE user_id = ? AND profile_id = ? AND ciphertext IS NOT NULL`(바인딩 순서 updated_at, user_id, profile_id. 뒤에 ` AND <조건>`을 붙여 써요). 아무것도 import하지 않아요.
- Produces (`sync-worker/src/keys.ts`):
  - `checkKeyId(id: string): string` — `vault`·`rekey`·규칙 밖은 404 `not-found`
  - `listKeys(env: SyncEnv, ctx: AuthContext): Promise<{ keyVault: KeyVaultHead | null; keyBlobs: KeyBlobRow[] }>`
  - `getKeyBlob(env, ctx, profileId: string): Promise<KeyBlob>`
  - `createVault(env, ctx, raw: unknown, now: number): Promise<{ vaultRevision: number; updatedAt: number }>`
  - `putKeyBlob(env, ctx, profileId: string, raw: unknown, now: number): Promise<{ revision: number; updatedAt: number }>`
  - `deleteKeyBlob(env, ctx, profileId: string, now: number): Promise<{ deleted: boolean; revision: number }>`
  - `rekey(env, ctx, raw: unknown, now: number): Promise<{ vaultRevision: number; updatedAt: number }>`
  - `wipeKeys(env, ctx): Promise<{ deleted: { vault: boolean; blobs: number } }>`
- HTTP(설계 4.1 표 그대로): `GET /v1/keys`, `DELETE /v1/keys`, `PUT /v1/keys/vault`, `POST /v1/keys/rekey`, `GET|PUT|DELETE /v1/keys/:id`(로그 패턴 `/v1/keys/:id`).

`sync-worker/src/keys.test.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { harness, OTHER, type Caller } from '../test/harness';
import { doc } from '../test/docs';

// 서버는 암호문을 풀지 않으므로 모양만 맞는 지어낸 base64를 쓴다
const A = (n: number, c: string) => c.repeat(n);
const VID = A(24, 'V');
const VID2 = A(24, 'W');
const SALT = A(24, 'S');
const SALT2 = A(24, 'T');
const vaultBody = (o: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  vaultId: VID,
  kdf: 'PBKDF2-SHA256',
  iter: 600000,
  salt: SALT,
  verifier: { nonce: A(16, 'N'), ciphertext: A(56, 'C') },
  ...o,
});
const blobBody = (o: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  expectedRevision: 0,
  newRevision: 1,
  contentId: A(24, 'I'),
  vaultId: VID,
  vaultRevision: 1,
  nonce: A(16, 'N'),
  ciphertext: A(64, 'X'),
  ...o,
});
const rekeyBody = (blobs: { profileId: string; revision: number; contentId?: string }[], o: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  expectedVaultId: VID,
  expectedVaultRevision: 1,
  salt: SALT2,
  verifier: { nonce: A(16, 'M'), ciphertext: A(56, 'D') },
  keyBlobs: blobs.map((b) => ({ profileId: b.profileId, revision: b.revision, contentId: b.contentId ?? A(24, 'I'), nonce: A(16, 'M'), ciphertext: A(64, 'R') })),
  ...o,
});

type H = ReturnType<typeof harness>;
const j = async (r: Response) => ({ status: r.status, body: (await r.json()) as Record<string, any> });
const rows = (h: H, sql: string) => h.env.DB.raw.prepare(sql).all() as Record<string, unknown>[];
const state = (h: H) => JSON.stringify([rows(h, 'SELECT * FROM key_vault ORDER BY user_id'), rows(h, 'SELECT * FROM key_blobs ORDER BY user_id, profile_id')]);
const blobRow = (h: H, p: string) => h.env.DB.raw.prepare('SELECT * FROM key_blobs WHERE profile_id = ?').get(p) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;

async function setup(profiles = ['p1']) {
  const h = harness();
  const me = await h.login();
  for (const p of profiles) expect((await me.as('PUT', `/v1/profiles/${p}`, { opId: `op-${p}`, expectedRevision: 0, doc: doc() })).status).toBe(200);
  expect((await me.as('PUT', '/v1/keys/vault', vaultBody())).status).toBe(200);
  return { h, me };
}
const put = (me: Caller, p: string, o: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => me.as('PUT', `/v1/keys/${p}`, blobBody(o));

describe('키 API 라우팅', () => {
  it('vault·rekey는 :id보다 먼저, 다른 메서드는 404이고 로그 패턴은 /v1/keys/:id, 두 DELETE는 본문 없이 200', async () => {
    const { h, me } = await setup();
    expect((await me.as('GET', '/v1/keys/vault')).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await me.as('PUT', '/v1/keys/rekey', blobBody())).status).toBe(404);
    expect(h.logs.some((l) => l.startsWith('GET /v1/keys/:id 404'))).toBe(true);
    expect(h.logs.some((l) => l.startsWith('PUT /v1/keys/:id 404'))).toBe(true);
    expect(await j(await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys/p1'))).toEqual({ status: 200, body: { deleted: false, revision: 0 } });
    expect(await j(await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys'))).toEqual({ status: 200, body: { deleted: { vault: true, blobs: 0 } } });
    expect((await h.call('GET', '/v1/keys')).status).toBe(401);
  });
});

describe('금고', () => {
  it('만들기 200, 같은 요청 다시 200, 다른 vaultId는 409 vault-exists(머리 포함), iter 310000은 422', async () => {
    const h = harness();
    const me = await h.login();
    const first = await j(await me.as('PUT', '/v1/keys/vault', vaultBody()));
    expect(first).toEqual({ status: 200, body: { vaultRevision: 1, updatedAt: h.clock.now } });
    expect((await j(await me.as('PUT', '/v1/keys/vault', vaultBody()))).status).toBe(200);
    const other = await j(await me.as('PUT', '/v1/keys/vault', vaultBody({ vaultId: VID2 })));
    expect(other.status).toBe(409);
    expect(other.body).toEqual({
      error: 'vault-exists',
      keyVault: { vaultId: VID, kdf: 'PBKDF2-SHA256', iter: 600000, salt: SALT, verifier: { nonce: A(16, 'N'), ciphertext: A(56, 'C') }, vaultRevision: 1, updatedAt: h.clock.now },
    });
    expect(await j(await me.as('PUT', '/v1/keys/vault', vaultBody({ iter: 310000 })))).toEqual({ status: 422, body: { error: 'invalid', path: 'iter' } });
    expect(await j(await me.as('PUT', '/v1/keys/vault', { ...vaultBody(), extra: 1 }))).toEqual({ status: 422, body: { error: 'unknown-field', path: 'extra' } });
  });

  it('지우고 다시 만든 금고: 옛 vaultId로 보낸 블롭 PUT·rekey는 409 vault-changed이고 아무것도 바뀌지 않는다', async () => {
    const { h, me } = await setup();
    expect((await put(me, 'p1')).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys')).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await me.as('PUT', '/v1/keys/vault', vaultBody({ vaultId: VID2 }))).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await put(me, 'p1', { contentId: A(24, 'J'), vaultId: VID2 })).status).toBe(200);
    const before = state(h);
    const stale = await j(await put(me, 'p1', { expectedRevision: 1, newRevision: 2 }));
    expect(stale).toEqual({ status: 409, body: { error: 'vault-changed', vaultRevision: 1 } });
    const rk = await j(await me.as('POST', '/v1/keys/rekey', rekeyBody([{ profileId: 'p1', revision: 1, contentId: A(24, 'J') }])));
    expect(rk).toEqual({ status: 409, body: { error: 'vault-changed', vaultRevision: 1 } });
    expect(state(h)).toBe(before);
  });
});

describe('블롭 PUT', () => {
  it('없음 404, tombstone 410, 금고 없음 409 no-vault, 금고 리비전 다름 409 vault-changed, 리비전 다름 409 conflict', async () => {
    const { h, me } = await setup(['p1', 'p2']);
    expect((await j(await put(me, 'p9'))).status).toBe(404);
    await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/profiles/p2', { opId: 'd2', expectedRevision: 1 });
    const gone = await j(await put(me, 'p2'));
    expect(gone.status).toBe(410);
    expect(gone.body).toMatchObject({ error: 'gone', deletedAt: h.clock.now, revision: 2 });
    expect(typeof gone.body.purgeAt).toBe('number');
    expect(await j(await put(me, 'p1', { vaultRevision: 2 }))).toEqual({ status: 409, body: { error: 'vault-changed', vaultRevision: 1 } });
    expect((await put(me, 'p1')).status).toBe(200);
    expect(await j(await put(me, 'p1', { contentId: A(24, 'K'), nonce: A(16, 'O') }))).toEqual({ status: 409, body: { error: 'conflict', revision: 1, vaultRevision: 1, deleted: false } });
    await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys/p1');
    expect(await j(await put(me, 'p1', { expectedRevision: 1, newRevision: 2 }))).toEqual({ status: 409, body: { error: 'conflict', revision: 2, vaultRevision: 1, deleted: true } });
    await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys');
    expect(await j(await put(me, 'p1', { expectedRevision: 2, newRevision: 3 }))).toEqual({ status: 409, body: { error: 'no-vault', vaultRevision: null } });
  });

  it('같은 본문을 다시 보내면 200과 같은 리비전', async () => {
    const { me } = await setup();
    const a = await j(await put(me, 'p1'));
    const b = await j(await put(me, 'p1'));
    expect(a).toEqual(b);
    expect(a.body.revision).toBe(1);
  });

  it('newRevision: expectedRevision 이하·2^31 − 1 이상은 422, 되감긴 행(3)에 3→6은 받는다', async () => {
    const { h, me } = await setup();
    expect(await j(await put(me, 'p1', { newRevision: 0 }))).toEqual({ status: 422, body: { error: 'invalid', path: 'newRevision' } });
    expect((await j(await put(me, 'p1', { newRevision: 2 ** 31 - 1 }))).status).toBe(422);
    expect((await put(me, 'p1')).status).toBe(200);
    h.env.DB.raw.prepare("UPDATE key_blobs SET revision = 3 WHERE profile_id = 'p1'").run();
    expect(await j(await put(me, 'p1', { expectedRevision: 3, newRevision: 3, contentId: A(24, 'K') }))).toEqual({ status: 422, body: { error: 'invalid', path: 'newRevision' } });
    expect((await j(await put(me, 'p1', { expectedRevision: 3, newRevision: 6, contentId: A(24, 'K') }))).body.revision).toBe(6);
  });

  it('2^31 − 2인 행을 끄면 2^31 − 1이 되고 CHECK 안이다', async () => {
    const { h, me } = await setup();
    expect((await put(me, 'p1', { newRevision: 2 ** 31 - 2 })).status).toBe(200);
    expect(await j(await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys/p1'))).toEqual({ status: 200, body: { deleted: true, revision: 2 ** 31 - 1 } });
    expect(blobRow(h, 'p1')).toMatchObject({ revision: 2 ** 31 - 1, ciphertext: null });
  });

  it('살아 있는 블롭 51번째는 422 key-limit, 삭제 표시 행은 세지 않는다', async () => {
    const { h, me } = await setup();
    const uid = (rows(h, 'SELECT id FROM users')[0] as { id: string }).id;
    const insIndex = h.env.DB.raw.prepare("INSERT INTO profile_index (user_id, profile_id, revision, last_op_id, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, 1, ?, 0)");
    const insBlob = h.env.DB.raw.prepare('INSERT INTO key_blobs (user_id, profile_id, revision, vault_revision, content_id, nonce, ciphertext, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, 1, 1, ?, ?, ?, 0)');
    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) {
      insIndex.run(uid, `q${i}`, `op-q${i}`);
      insBlob.run(uid, `q${i}`, A(24, 'I'), A(16, 'N'), A(64, 'X'));
    }
    expect(await j(await put(me, 'p1'))).toEqual({ status: 422, body: { error: 'key-limit' } });
    expect((await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys/q0')).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await put(me, 'p1')).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it('암호문 2049자는 422, 8 KiB를 넘는 본문은 413', async () => {
    const { me } = await setup();
    expect((await put(me, 'p1', { ciphertext: A(2049, 'X') })).status).toBe(422);
    expect((await put(me, 'p1', { ciphertext: A(9000, 'X') })).status).toBe(413);
  });
});

describe('끄기·목록·조회', () => {
  it('끄기: 암호문 NULL·리비전 + 1, 다시 켜면 이어지고, 다시 보내면 deleted false', async () => {
    const { h, me } = await setup();
    await put(me, 'p1');
    expect(await j(await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys/p1'))).toEqual({ status: 200, body: { deleted: true, revision: 2 } });
    expect(blobRow(h, 'p1')).toMatchObject({ revision: 2, content_id: null, nonce: null, ciphertext: null });
    expect(await j(await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys/p1'))).toEqual({ status: 200, body: { deleted: false, revision: 2 } });
    expect((await j(await put(me, 'p1', { expectedRevision: 2, newRevision: 3 }))).body.revision).toBe(3);
  });

  it('목록: 삭제된 프로필의 행은 없고, 끈 행은 deleted true·contentId null, 암호문·nonce가 없다', async () => {
    const { h, me } = await setup(['p1', 'p2', 'p3']);
    await put(me, 'p1');
    await put(me, 'p2', { contentId: A(24, 'J') });
    await put(me, 'p3', { contentId: A(24, 'K') });
    await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys/p2');
    await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/profiles/p3', { opId: 'd3', expectedRevision: 1 });
    const res = await me.as('GET', '/v1/keys');
    const text = await res.clone().text();
    expect(text).not.toContain(A(64, 'X'));
    expect(text).not.toContain('"ciphertext":"X');
    const { body } = await j(res);
    expect(body.keyVault).toMatchObject({ vaultId: VID, vaultRevision: 1 });
    expect(body.keyBlobs).toEqual([
      { profileId: 'p1', revision: 1, vaultRevision: 1, contentId: A(24, 'I'), deleted: false, updatedAt: h.clock.now },
      { profileId: 'p2', revision: 2, vaultRevision: 1, contentId: null, deleted: true, updatedAt: h.clock.now },
    ]);
    expect(await j(await me.as('GET', '/v1/keys/p1'))).toEqual({
      status: 200,
      body: { revision: 1, contentId: A(24, 'I'), vaultRevision: 1, nonce: A(16, 'N'), ciphertext: A(64, 'X'), updatedAt: h.clock.now },
    });
    expect((await me.as('GET', '/v1/keys/p2')).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await me.as('GET', '/v1/keys/p3')).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it('금고가 없으면 keyVault null, keyBlobs []', async () => {
    const h = harness();
    const me = await h.login();
    expect(await j(await me.as('GET', '/v1/keys'))).toEqual({ status: 200, body: { keyVault: null, keyBlobs: [] } });
  });
});

describe('rekey', () => {
  async function two() {
    const s = await setup(['p1', 'p2', 'p3']);
    await put(s.me, 'p1');
    await put(s.me, 'p2', { contentId: A(24, 'J') });
    return s;
  }
  const both = [{ profileId: 'p1', revision: 1 }, { profileId: 'p2', revision: 1, contentId: A(24, 'J') }];

  it('성공: 살아 있는 블롭의 vault_revision만 새 값, revision·content_id·vault_id 그대로, key_guard는 비고, 다시 보내도 200', async () => {
    const { h, me } = await two();
    const r = await j(await me.as('POST', '/v1/keys/rekey', rekeyBody(both)));
    expect(r).toEqual({ status: 200, body: { vaultRevision: 2, updatedAt: h.clock.now } });
    expect(rows(h, 'SELECT profile_id, revision, vault_revision, content_id, ciphertext FROM key_blobs ORDER BY profile_id')).toEqual([
      { profile_id: 'p1', revision: 1, vault_revision: 2, content_id: A(24, 'I'), ciphertext: A(64, 'R') },
      { profile_id: 'p2', revision: 1, vault_revision: 2, content_id: A(24, 'J'), ciphertext: A(64, 'R') },
    ]);
    expect(rows(h, 'SELECT vault_id, vault_revision, salt FROM key_vault')).toEqual([{ vault_id: VID, vault_revision: 2, salt: SALT2 }]);
    expect(rows(h, 'SELECT count(*) AS n FROM key_guard')).toEqual([{ n: 0 }]);
    expect((await j(await me.as('POST', '/v1/keys/rekey', rekeyBody(both)))).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it.each([
    ['그사이 블롭이 고쳐짐', async (me: Caller) => void (await put(me, 'p1', { expectedRevision: 1, newRevision: 2, contentId: A(24, 'L') }))],
    ['요청에 하나가 빠짐', async () => {}],
    ['그사이 새 블롭이 켜짐', async (me: Caller) => void (await put(me, 'p3', { contentId: A(24, 'M') }))],
    ['리비전은 같고 content_id가 다름', async () => {}],
  ])('경합 — %s: 409 conflict이고 금고·블롭이 하나도 바뀌지 않는다', async (name, race) => {
    const { h, me } = await two();
    await race(me);
    const before = state(h);
    const req =
      name === '요청에 하나가 빠짐'
        ? rekeyBody([both[0]])
        : name === '리비전은 같고 content_id가 다름'
          ? rekeyBody([both[0], { ...both[1], contentId: A(24, 'Z') }])
          : rekeyBody(both);
    const r = await j(await me.as('POST', '/v1/keys/rekey', req));
    expect(r.status).toBe(409);
    expect(r.body.error).toBe('conflict');
    expect(r.body.keyVault).toMatchObject({ vaultId: VID, vaultRevision: 1 });
    expect(Array.isArray(r.body.keyBlobs)).toBe(true);
    expect(JSON.stringify(r.body)).not.toContain(A(64, 'X'));
    expect(state(h)).toBe(before);
    expect(rows(h, 'SELECT count(*) AS n FROM key_guard')).toEqual([{ n: 0 }]);
  });

  it('삭제된 프로필에 암호문이 남은 고아 행이 있어도 성공하고, 고아는 삭제 표시가 된다', async () => {
    const { h, me } = await two();
    await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/profiles/p2', { opId: 'd2', expectedRevision: 1 });
    // 되돌린 Worker 기간의 고아: tombstone이 키 행을 바꾸지 못했다고 친다
    h.env.DB.raw.prepare("UPDATE key_blobs SET revision = 1, content_id = ?, nonce = ?, ciphertext = ? WHERE profile_id = 'p2'").run(A(24, 'J'), A(16, 'N'), A(64, 'X'));
    expect((await me.as('POST', '/v1/keys/rekey', rekeyBody([both[0]]))).status).toBe(200);
    expect(blobRow(h, 'p2')).toMatchObject({ revision: 2, ciphertext: null, content_id: null, nonce: null });
  });

  it('블롭 50개 rekey가 통과한다(128 KiB 안)', async () => {
    const { h, me } = await setup();
    const uid = (rows(h, 'SELECT id FROM users')[0] as { id: string }).id;
    const insIndex = h.env.DB.raw.prepare("INSERT INTO profile_index (user_id, profile_id, revision, last_op_id, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, 1, ?, 0)");
    const insBlob = h.env.DB.raw.prepare('INSERT INTO key_blobs (user_id, profile_id, revision, vault_revision, content_id, nonce, ciphertext, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, 1, 1, ?, ?, ?, 0)');
    const list = Array.from({ length: 50 }, (_, i) => ({ profileId: `q${i}`, revision: 1 }));
    for (const b of list) {
      insIndex.run(uid, b.profileId, `op-${b.profileId}`);
      insBlob.run(uid, b.profileId, A(24, 'I'), A(16, 'N'), A(64, 'X'));
    }
    const big = rekeyBody(list);
    for (const k of big.keyBlobs) k.ciphertext = A(2048, 'R');
    expect((await me.as('POST', '/v1/keys/rekey', big)).status).toBe(200);
    expect(rows(h, 'SELECT count(*) AS n FROM key_blobs WHERE vault_revision = 2')).toEqual([{ n: 50 }]);
  });

  it('금고가 없으면 409 no-vault, 본문 검사 실패는 422', async () => {
    const h = harness();
    const me = await h.login();
    expect((await j(await me.as('POST', '/v1/keys/rekey', rekeyBody([])))).body).toEqual({ error: 'no-vault' });
    expect((await j(await me.as('POST', '/v1/keys/rekey', { ...rekeyBody([]), extra: 1 }))).status).toBe(422);
  });
});

describe('전체 삭제·사용자 경계·로그', () => {
  it('전체 삭제: 금고·블롭이 모두 지워지고 다시 보내도 200, 다른 사용자 것은 남는다', async () => {
    const { h, me } = await setup();
    await put(me, 'p1');
    const other = await h.login(OTHER, 'uid-other');
    await other.as('PUT', '/v1/profiles/o1', { opId: 'op-o1', expectedRevision: 0, doc: doc() });
    await other.as('PUT', '/v1/keys/vault', vaultBody());
    await other.as('PUT', '/v1/keys/o1', blobBody());
    expect(await j(await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys'))).toEqual({ status: 200, body: { deleted: { vault: true, blobs: 1 } } });
    expect(await j(await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys'))).toEqual({ status: 200, body: { deleted: { vault: false, blobs: 0 } } });
    expect(rows(h, 'SELECT count(*) AS n FROM key_vault')).toEqual([{ n: 1 }]);
    expect(rows(h, 'SELECT profile_id FROM key_blobs')).toEqual([{ profile_id: 'o1' }]);
  });

  it('다른 계정 세션으로 GET·PUT·DELETE해도 내 블롭이 보이거나 바뀌지 않는다', async () => {
    const { h, me } = await setup();
    await put(me, 'p1');
    const before = state(h);
    const other = await h.login(OTHER, 'uid-other');
    expect(await j(await other.as('GET', '/v1/keys'))).toEqual({ status: 200, body: { keyVault: null, keyBlobs: [] } });
    expect((await other.as('GET', '/v1/keys/p1')).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await other.as('PUT', '/v1/keys/p1', blobBody({ expectedRevision: 1, newRevision: 2 }))).status).toBe(404);
    expect(await j(await other.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys/p1'))).toEqual({ status: 200, body: { deleted: false, revision: 0 } });
    expect(state(h)).toBe(before);
  });

  it('로그 어느 줄에도 ciphertext·salt·nonce·vaultId·contentId 문자열이 없다', async () => {
    const { h, me } = await setup();
    await put(me, 'p1');
    await me.as('GET', '/v1/keys');
    await me.as('GET', '/v1/keys/p1');
    await me.as('POST', '/v1/keys/rekey', rekeyBody([{ profileId: 'p1', revision: 1 }]));
    await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys/p1');
    await me.as('DELETE', '/v1/keys');
    const log = h.logs.join('\n');
    for (const secret of [A(64, 'X'), A(64, 'R'), SALT, SALT2, A(16, 'N'), A(16, 'M'), VID, A(24, 'I')]) expect(log).not.toContain(secret);
    expect(h.logs.some((l) => l.startsWith('POST /v1/keys/rekey 200'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('공유 API는 key_ 표를 읽지 않는다(shares.ts 원문에 key_vault·key_blobs가 없다)', () => {
    const src = readFileSync(new URL('./shares.ts', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
    expect(src).not.toContain('key_vault');
    expect(src).not.toContain('key_blobs');
  });
});
```
